# Re:Zero — master longitudinal ledger

## 0. Authority, history, and use

This is the canonical cumulative state ledger for the Re:Zero Japanese main-light-novel analysis. It is mutable current authority through the latest verified volume transaction. Frozen volume readings remain the authority for what was supportable at each historical prospective horizon and for their detailed source-facing arguments.

This ledger was created after V02 during the project-initiation architecture repair. The V01/V02 state below is a controlled backfill from the already frozen canonical readings; the ledger did not govern those readings prospectively and neither freeze was modified. Exact source evidence was consulted only where necessary to validate the source identities already recorded by those artifacts.

For every new volume, update this file only after the source-facing reading has identified the material transitions. Preserve revision ancestry and route to the relevant VNN section or exact EPUB locus. Do not duplicate an independently promoted ledger here beyond a routing/current-summary row.

## 12. Prospective question horizon

### Historical entering V04 horizon

The 24 questions below were all OPEN before V04. They remain here as the entering record, not the present unanswered register. V04 §14 explicitly adjudicates every row; §12 adjudicates predecessor claims. Current OPEN burdens and new V05 tests follow the historical table.

| question ID | bounded V04 question |
|---|---|
| `RZ-Q-V04-001` | Does the next observed restoration, if any, retain or move the mansion checkpoint? |
| `RZ-Q-V04-002` | What determines the disclosure enforcer's growing embodiment and semantic trigger boundary? |
| `RZ-Q-V04-003` | Does Witch odor decay, remain, or become intentionally controllable without further punishment? |
| `RZ-Q-V04-004` | Does the black feminine figure recur, and can `──いしてる` be completed or attributed safely? |
| `RZ-Q-V04-005` | Do Subaru's inert curse formulas create later symptoms, detection, or removal opportunities? |
| `RZ-Q-V04-006` | How long does gate damage constrain magic, and does attempted casting worsen it? |
| `RZ-Q-V04-007` | Who is the brown-braided mabeast handler, and to whom is she answerable? |
| `RZ-Q-V04-008` | Was the operational target Subaru, Emilia, Roswaal, the village, or estate stability? |
| `RZ-Q-V04-009` | What was the purpose of the Garfiel lure, and who or what is Garfiel in the opened main-LN account? |
| `RZ-Q-V04-010` | Is there evidence of a shared sponsor or coordination between the handler and Elsa? |
| `RZ-Q-V04-011` | Does the royal-selection setting verify, revise, or reject Roswaal's sabotage inference? |

### Post-V04 architecture-promotion review

The preceding paragraph describes the historical V03 review, not current opening state. At V04, five camps and the distinct qualification/consent/office/enforcement/resource models earn the reserved `REZERO_INSTITUTIONS_FACTIONS_AND_POWER_LEDGER.md`. Former §8 state/history migrates there and is no longer maintained twice. The architecture/schema/map are synchronized in this volume transaction.

Event-state remains one continued line; knowledge and relationship tables have more rows but still safely expose holder/history differences; mechanics retains attribution/exception control; ordinary life and Japanese evidence remain retrievable through VNN loci. These dimensions `RETAIN IN MASTER`; dedicated readiness/evidence indices are `NOT CURRENTLY WARRANTED`. Mature character, relationship and final specialist promotion remain separate thresholds. No capital-arc endpoint is claimed from its source-verified beginning.

Current metadata correction: the V03 review formerly counted five V02 states instead of four and used an inconsistent readiness-row tally. The compact chronology and frozen artifacts were never changed; this mutable review now uses its actual state count and avoids an ambiguous grouped-person census.

### Post-V05 architecture-promotion review

V05 expands the existing institution model rather than creating another political home. Event-state adds E1/E2/E3 with exact checkpoint, timing, durability and carry-forward; knowledge adds collapse/odor, hidden mission, cult claims and unknown beast; relationships add Rem/Emilia, host variants and Otto.
